Drawing on evidence from cognitive science, information theory, evolutionary biology, software engineering, medicine, legal reasoning, education, music, and artificial intelligence, the paper develops the concept of atomic units as fundamental compression layers that support efficiency, transfer, interpretability, and evolvability. The central contribution is the Compression Calculus, a formal framework for comparing surface-level representations with atomic representations and for describing how compression gains compound across abstraction layers. We introduce the Compounding Cascade thesis, according to which each additional layer of abstraction multiplicatively increases representational efficiency rather than merely adding incremental savings. The paper further argues that contemporary AI systems often operate at suboptimal levels of representation, relying on token-level processing or document-level retrieval rather than stable, concept-level atomic structures. In this view, large language models are best understood not as complete knowledge architectures, but as dynamic fusion engines capable of navigating, sequencing, and recombining atomic units. The framework provides a foundation for designing self-evolving knowledge systems that can discover, refine, and compose new primitives over time. By reframing intelligence as compression through compositional abstraction, the paper offers a unifying perspective on expertise, knowledge representation, explainable AI, and the future architecture of adaptive intelligent systems.

</details>


### [176] [A Learning-Rate-Gated Failure of GRPO in a Small Language and Vision-Language Model Web Agent: A Controlled Null and Its Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12640)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,Shiwen Ni,Qinghao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了GRPO（组相对策略优化）在4B-8B规模语言-视觉智能体上的效果。通过18组控制实验发现，GRPO在强监督基线上无法提升已掌握任务的成功率，仅在存在头部空间（即采样策略已比贪婪策略更优）时有效。中学习率会退化模型，高学习率则导致崩溃，这两种机制呈现双重分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GRPO是否能为小规模（4B-8B）多模态网络智能体带来真正技能提升，还是仅仅重塑监督模型已有的行为模式。

Method: 在18组控制实验网格中变化学习率、KL权重、种子、初始化和裁剪参数，分别在文本和Set-of-Marks截图观察条件下测试，并对比了可达奖励任务与不可达任务的效果差异。

Result: GRPO在强监督基线上无法提升已掌握任务的成功率，但在存在头部空间的任务上成功率达+22。中学习率导致模型退化（可定位到注意力与MLP块），高学习率导致崩溃（无法归因于单一模块）。4B模型有效秩与能力耦合，8B模型则分离。

Conclusion: GRPO仅在采样策略已有提升空间时才有帮助，否则可能造成退化或崩溃。小模型（4B）中有效秩跟踪能力变化，而大模型（8B）中两者解耦，这一现象具有尺度依赖性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) in particular, is now run routinely on a supervised checkpoint in the hope of producing a stronger agent. We ask whether it adds skill to a small language and vision-language model web agent at the 4B to 8B scale, or whether it mostly reshapes behavior the supervised model already has. Across a control grid of 18 runs that varies learning rate, KL weight, seed, initialization, and clipping, no configuration credibly improves the success rate of a strong supervised baseline on tasks the agent has largely mastered. On the text track, moderate to high learning rates make it credibly worse. The null holds under paired testing, 25 evaluation seeds, 6 training seeds, changes to the recipe, both text and Set-of-Marks screenshot observations, and scaling the backbone to 8B; the credible harm is a text-track finding and is only nominal under Set-of-Marks. To show that the null reflects the setting and not a broken pipeline, we run the identical harness, reward, and recipe on tasks whose reward is reachable by sampling, and there the success rate rises by 22 points with a paired interval that excludes zero. GRPO therefore helps only when there is headroom to climb, meaning the sampled policy already succeeds more often than the greedy one. We then explain the failure. A middle learning rate degrades the agent and a high one collapses it, and the two regimes form a double dissociation: grafting localizes the degrade regime to the attention and MLP blocks, while the collapse regime cannot be traced to any single group, and the embedding change that dominates the weight movement is causally inert. At 4B, effective rank in the late layers tracks capability in both directions; at 8B the two come apart. This coupling is specific to the smaller model, so we report it as scale-dependent.

</details>


### [177] [Internet of Agentic Things: Networked AI Agents for Closed-Loop IoT Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12662)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出物联网代理化（IoAT）架构，将代理AI、IoT、网络物理系统、物理AI、边缘计算和数字孪生统一为闭环编排框架，并形式化为耦合的工作流-控制问题，通过hylomorphic动态规划框架连接代理规划与物理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IoT、AI和网络物理系统多独立发展，缺乏统一的代理化闭环编排框架来融合感知、推理、协调和驱动能力，亟需一种能够跨分布式网络物理环境自主协作的架构。

Method: 提出三层架构（云、边缘/雾、物理IoT层），通过自主AI代理连接；形式化为耦合工作流-控制问题，采用hylomorphic动态规划框架实现嵌套的战略与战术决策。

Result: 以智能建筑编排为代表性用例展示了框架的有效性，并系统梳理了安全、治理、韧性、可信部署等关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: IoAT为代理AI与物理系统的深度集成提供了统一的理论框架和实践路径，但需进一步解决安全、治理和可信性等关键问题才能实现可靠部署。

Abstract: The paper introduces the Internet of Agentic Things (IoAT), an architectural framework that integrates agentic AI, IoT, cyber-physical systems, Physical AI, edge computing, and digital twins into a unified closed-loop orchestration framework. The proposed architecture consists of cloud, edge/fog, and physical IoT layers connected through autonomous AI agents that perceive, reason, coordinate, and actuate across distributed cyber-physical environments. The paper formalizes IoAT as a coupled workflow-control problem with nested strategic and tactical decision making using a hylomorphic dynamic programming framework that links agentic planning with physical execution. Smart-building orchestration is presented as a representative use case, and key research challenges related to safety, security, governance, resilience, and trustworthy deployment are discussed.

</details>


### [178] [MaxSAT-Based Feedback for Guiding Vision-Language Models in Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12711)
*Pedro Orvalho,Guillem Alenyà,Felip Manyà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合MaxSAT约束求解的神经符号方法，将VLM生成的数独候选解通过软硬子句编码，利用MaxSAT求解器检测并修正逻辑不一致性，从而提升视觉语言模型在结构化推理任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在结构化视觉推理任务中虽具备强感知能力，但缺乏显式的逻辑一致性约束机制，常产生违反底层约束的赋值结果，亟需一种能保证逻辑一致性的推理增强方法。

Method: 采用神经符号方法，将VLM生成的候选放置编码为部分MaxSAT公式中的软子句，数独约束保持为硬子句；当不一致时，MaxSAT求解器找出最大一致子集并转换为结构化文本和视觉反馈，引导VLM进行后续修正。

Result: 在多个开源和闭源VLM上的数独数据集实验表明，基于MaxSAT的反馈显著提升了逻辑一致性，并增加了成功求解的实例数量，尤其在全局棋盘修正模式下效果更佳。

Conclusion: 符号优化能够有效增强视觉语言推理的可靠性，证明了将形式化约束推理融入VLM处理流程的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Vision--Language Models (VLMs) have recently demonstrated promising performance on structured visual reasoning tasks, including grid-based puzzles. However, despite strong perceptual capabilities, these models lack explicit mechanisms for enforcing logical consistency and frequently generate assignments that violate underlying constraints. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic approach that integrates formal constraint reasoning into the VLM solving process via a Maximum Satisfiability (MaxSAT) oracle. Rather than computing solutions directly, the symbolic component acts as a consistency validator and refinement engine. Candidate placements generated by the VLM are encoded as soft clauses in a partial MaxSAT formulation, while Sudoku constraints remain hard clauses. When inconsistencies arise, the MaxSAT solver identifies a largest mutually consistent subset of assignments, which is then translated into structured textual and visual feedback to guide subsequent refinements. We evaluate our approach on a Sudoku dataset across multiple open-source and closed-access VLMs. Results show that MaxSAT-based feedback improves logical consistency and increases the number of solved instances, particularly in full-board refinement mode. These findings demonstrate that symbolic optimisation can enhance the reliability of vision-language reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [LLMs Can See the Smoke but not the Fire: Evaluating Abductive Reasoning with Elenchos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12733)
*Julius Steiglechner,Lucas Mahler,Gabriele Lohmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Elenchos框架，将溯因推理建模为结构逆问题，发现LLMs在检测系统变异方面表现较好，但在识别具体突变规则上存在显著困难，尤其是面对交互突变时表现更差，且增加推理时间的收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在模式识别和文本生成方面表现出色，但它们进行溯因推理（从观测行为推断潜在假设）的能力尚不明确，现有评估方法缺乏对这一能力的系统性度量。

Method: 引入Elenchos（源自苏格拉底式诘问法）生成式评估框架，将溯因推理形式化为结构逆问题：以参考形式系统（如lambda演算）及其潜在变异版本为输入，要求模型判断是否发生突变并推断导致行为差异的规则修改。

Result: 前沿和中端LLMs存在检测-归因分离现象：模型能识别系统被改变，但难以确定潜在突变；交互突变条件下性能显著下降，模型常只能恢复部分突变；增加推理时间带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在溯因推理任务中表现出检测与归因能力的不对称性，尤其在复杂交互突变场景下存在明显局限，且通过增加推理时计算资源难以有效弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at pattern recognition and text generation, but their capacity for abductive inference - inferring latent hypotheses that explain observed behavior - remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Elenchos (named after the Socratic method of cross-examination), a generative evaluation framework that measures abductive reasoning as a structural inverse problem. Given a reference formal system, such as the lambda-calculus, and a potentially mutated counterpart, agents must determine whether a mutation has occurred and infer the rule modifications responsible for the resulting behavioral differences. Evaluating frontier and mid-tier LLMs reveals a consistent detection-attribution dissociation: models often recognize that a system has been altered but struggle to identify the latent mutations causing the observed discrepancies. Performance degrades substantially under interacting mutations, where models frequently recover only a subset of the underlying mutations. Preliminary evidence also suggests diminishing returns from increased inference-time reasoning, with only modest improvements under larger reasoning budgets, though this finding requires further validation.

</details>


### [180] [Tracing Agentic Failure from the Flow of Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12747)
*Samuel Yeh,Yiwen Zhu,Shaleen Deep,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OAT方法，利用神经受控微分方程对成功轨迹进行一类学习，在推理时通过异常检测实现无监督的智能体系统故障归因，相比基于提示的方法速度快200-5000倍且F1分数提升20%（域内）/7%（域外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体系统故障归因方法计算成本高（基于提示的流水线）或需要昂贵且难以扩展的步级错误标注进行后训练，亟需一种轻量级、无需步级监督的实用故障归因模型。

Method: 将故障归因转化为一类学习问题，利用神经受控微分方程对成功轨迹的动力学模式进行建模；推理时计算失败轨迹中各步相对于所学成功动力学的异常分数，识别错误步骤。

Result: 仅需100条成功轨迹训练，速度比基于提示的基线快200-5000倍；域内数据集F1分数提升+20%，域外数据集提升+7%，持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: OAT是一种高效且有前景的智能体系统故障诊断方向，实现了轻量级、无需步级监督的准确故障归因。

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM-based agentic systems, i.e., identifying which steps in a failure trajectory caused the task to fail, is critical for debugging and improving these systems. Existing approaches either rely on prompting-based pipelines, which are computationally expensive, or require post-training on failure trajectories with step-level error annotations, which are costly to collect and difficult to scale. We argue that a practical failure attribution model should be lightweight and trainable without step-level supervision on failure data. To this end, we address unsupervised failure attribution, i.e., training exclusively on successful trajectories and identifying error steps at inference time given a failure trajectory. We propose OAT, which casts this problem as one-class learning with neural controlled differential equations, modeling the dynamical pattern of successful trajectories in latent space. At inference time, each step in a failure trajectory is assigned an anomaly score based on its deviation from the dynamics learned on successful trajectories, which is then used to form a set of error steps. With training on only 100 successful trajectories, experiments show that OAT is 200--5000 $\times$ faster than prompting-based baselines, and, at the same time, consistently outperforms them in both in-domain and out-of-distribution datasets with +20% and +7% F1 scores, respectively, demonstrating that OAT is a promising and efficient direction for diagnosing agentic system failures.

</details>


### [181] [Accuracy and Normalized Accuracy under Length Bias: Analysis, Guidelines, and a Bayesian Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12767)
*Koen Oostermeijer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出基于条件对数概率的多选基准存在长度偏差（长答案被惩罚），而长度归一化会过度纠正导致反向偏差。作者提出贝叶斯准确率，通过显式长度先验计算后验概率，消除线性长度效应，在多个基准上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选基准中，基于条件对数概率的评分存在长度偏差：长答案因token求和而被低估。常用的长度归一化启发式方法又过度纠正，引入偏向长答案的反向偏差。需要一种更合理的评分方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯准确率（Bayesian accuracy），在答案长度上引入显式先验分布，计算每个候选答案的后验概率，从而消除线性长度效应。该方法无需额外前向传播，可直接替代基于似然的多选评估。

Result: 经验证明，标准准确率偏向短答案，长度归一化准确率偏向长答案，而贝叶斯准确率在多个基准和少样本设置下始终表现出更低的经验长度偏差。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯准确率是一种有效的drop-in替代方案，能缓解多选评估中的长度偏差问题，且不增加计算成本。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks that rank candidate completions by conditional log-probability suffer from a length bias: because log-probabilities sum over tokens, longer answers tend to be penalized relative to shorter ones in practice. A common mitigation is to normalize scores by completion length, but we show empirically that this heuristic frequently over-corrects, introducing a bias toward longer answers instead. We first analyze these scoring rules, characterizing when standard and length-normalized accuracy are appropriate and how their length biases depend on the distribution of completion lengths. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce \emph{Bayesian accuracy}, a scoring rule that computes the posterior probability of each candidate under an explicit prior over answer length, thereby removing linear length effects. Bayesian accuracy is a drop-in replacement for likelihood-based multiple-choice evaluation, requires no additional forward passes, and consistently exhibits lower empirical length bias than both standard and length-normalized accuracy across benchmarks and few-shot settings.

</details>


### [182] [Do We Really Need Multimodal Emotion Language Models Larger Than 1B Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12787)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Junchen Fu,Wenhao Deng,Hu Han,Joemon M. Jose,Xuri Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级多模态情感识别框架Light-MER，通过知识蒸馏将大模型知识迁移至亚十亿参数学生模型，结合切片Wasserstein距离隐藏状态对齐和基于GRPO的多奖励优化策略，在9个基准数据集上实现SOTA性能与推理效率的显著提升，挑战了MER领域大模型必要性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在提升多模态情感识别性能的同时，模型参数量激增（至少7B），导致高计算成本和低推理效率，难以在机器人、移动设备等资源受限平台上实时部署。本文质疑是否真的需要超过1B参数的大模型来实现高质量MER。

Method: 提出Light-MER轻量级框架，通过知识蒸馏将大规模教师模型的知识迁移至亚十亿参数学生模型。引入两种优化策略：(1) 结合切片Wasserstein距离与隐藏状态对齐的最优传输损失；(2) 基于GRPO的多奖励优化策略，平衡MER性能与效率。

Result: 在9个基准数据集上，Light-MER实现了SOTA性能，同时显著提升了推理效率，证明小规模多模态情感语言模型具有强大潜力。

Conclusion: 挑战了MER领域"更大模型必然更好"的假设，证实通过有效的知识蒸馏和优化策略，亚十亿参数模型可以兼顾高性能和高效率，为资源受限平台上的实时MER部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have significantly improved the performance of multimodal emotion recognition (MER) and enabled interpretable description generation by jointly modeling video, audio, and language, etc. However, these performance improvements are often accompanied by an increase in model parameter size (e.g, at least 7B), which simultaneously incurs high computational costs and reduces inference efficiency, thereby hindering real-time deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as robots and mobile devices. This raises a fundamental question: do we really need the multimodal MER model larger than 1B parameters for high-quality MER?
  In this paper, we challenge the assumption that larger models are inherently necessary and proposes a lightweight MER framework (called Light-MER), which achieves better and faster multimodal sentiment understanding and recognition through knowledge distillation. It can transfer knowledge from a strong, large-scale teacher model to a lightweight sub-billion-parameter student model, aiming to preserve rich multimodal emotion reasoning and recognition while substantially improving deployment efficiency. Specifically, we introduce two new optimization strategies to enhance knowledge transfer: (1) a new optimal transport loss that combines Sliced Wasserstein Distance with hidden-state alignment, and (2) a new multi-reward optimization strategy based on GRPO that balances MER performance and efficiency, aimed at further enhancing the learning capabilities of student models. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets demonstrate that Light-MER achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly improving inference efficiency. This highlights the strong potential of small multimodal emotion language models for future research. Code is available at https://github.com/GAIR-Lab/Light-MER.

</details>


### [183] [Who Grades the Grader? Co-Evolving Evaluation Metrics and Skills for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12790)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无可靠自动评估指标下的自我进化智能体系统新范式：通过共同进化评估指标与技能生命周期（Double Ratchet），实现无需预定义指标的自主改进，并引入锚定约束与外部审计确保安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体系统隐式假设存在可靠的评估指标，但现实应用中常缺乏此类指标。本文旨在消除这一假设，提出可在无可靠自动验证器场景下运行的进化架构。

Method: 提出Double Ratchet架构：(1) 指标进化循环：通过组合小型缺陷检测器，在完整进化生命周期下训练，与10项锚定参照集对齐，并通过未标注输出上的共识进行正则化，产出透明可审计的指标；(2) 指标与技能生命周期共进化：指标与技能循环协同进化，以锚定参照集为标尺；(3) 安全机制：锚定约束防止指标坍塌，外部审计检测技能对指标的利用行为。

Result: 在代码生成（MBPP+）、企业级Text-to-SQL（Spider 2.0-Snow）和无参考报告生成任务上，Double Ratchet保留了由真实指标驱动时88-110%的保留提升效果。消融实验表明移除锚定防护导致指标坍塌，而移除生命周期则无此现象。当进化技能利用报告指标时，独立法官可检测到该行为，一个检测器可修复，任务感知法官在77%的判定对中偏好进化后的输出。

Conclusion: 在缺乏可靠自动验证器的场景下，这种"预设失败"架构应作为默认选择。通过共同进化评估指标与技能生命周期，配合锚定约束与外部审计，可在无可靠指标条件下实现安全的自我进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agent systems improve by creating, revising, and retiring their own skills, but every such loop rests on a hidden assumption: a reliable evaluation metric already exists. In many real applications it does not. We make three claims. First, metrics can be \emph{evolved}: our metric loop searches compositions of small drawback detectors under a full evolutionary lifecycle, trained to agree with a ten-item anchored reference set, regularized by consensus over unlabeled outputs, and audited against a held-out anchor it never reads, yielding a transparent, inspectable metric rather than an opaque judge. Second, since no metric exists to beat, the yardstick is recovering what an accurate metric would have enabled, and \emph{Double Ratchet}, our co-evolution of the metric with a lifecycle-managed skill loop, does so: across code generation (MBPP+), enterprise text-to-SQL (Spider~2.0-Snow), and reference-free report generation, it retains 88--110\% of the held-out lift achieved by the same skill loop driven by ground truth or the best available rubric. Third, safety comes from anchor discipline plus outer audits: removing anchor guards collapses the metric into a vacuous detector while removing the lifecycle does not; and when evolved skills gamed the report rubric, an independent judge caught it, one detector repaired it, and a task-aware judge then preferred the evolved outputs over the pre-evolution baseline in 77\% of decided pairs. We argue this failure-expecting architecture is the right default wherever no reliable automatic verifier exists.

</details>


### [184] [Visual Access Boundaries in Vision-Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12815)
*Hiroto Osaka,Shohei Taniguchi,Gouki Minegishi,Kai Yamashita,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预方法Visual Access Sweep，发现Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting在视觉-语言模型中的主要作用并非延长对图像token的直接访问，而是扩展基于图像派生隐藏状态的语言侧计算。CoT的性能提升受限于视觉属性的可读性（perceptual readout），而非计数能力本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CoT被广泛用于VLM的测试时扩展策略，但其内部机制尚不明确——不清楚CoT生成更长推理轨迹时，是在持续访问图像token，还是主要利用前向传播中已提取的视觉信息。

Method: 提出Visual Access Sweep因果干预方法，通过沿层深度和生成时间遮蔽生成token查询对图像token键的注意力，定义视觉访问边界（VAB）为保持任务准确率的最小访问区域。在Qwen2.5-VL和InternVL3的6个模型配置上，对比无CoT直接回答和CoT提示下的VAB差异。

Result: 1) 在Qwen2.5-VL-32B及InternVL3的14B/38B规模上，CoT与无CoT全访问目标的VAB层差异最多不超过2层，尽管CoT生成了更长的推理序列。2) CoT在模型能可靠读出的视觉属性上有效，在不可靠读出的属性上无效。3) 符号属性oracle实验表明，当以文本形式提供真实属性时CoT能提升计数能力；单目标probe-vs-decode检查显示，困难属性可从隐藏状态线性恢复但模型自身难以输出。

Conclusion: CoT并不主要通过延长对图像token的直接访问来提升性能，瓶颈在于感知读出的可靠性而非计数能力本身。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting is widely used as a test-time scaling strategy for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it remains unclear what is extended when VLMs generate longer reasoning traces. We ask whether CoT requires continued access to image tokens, or whether it mainly operates over visual information already made available earlier in the forward pass. We introduce Visual Access Sweep, a causal intervention that masks attention from generated-token queries to image-token keys along layer depth and generation time, and define the Visual Access Boundary (VAB) as the minimal access region that preserves task accuracy. Across six model configurations from Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, both no-CoT direct answering and CoT prompting exhibit finite VABs. In Qwen2.5-VL-32B and InternVL3 at 14B and 38B scales, when CoT is evaluated against the no-CoT full-access target, its VAB layer differs from the no-CoT boundary by at most two layers, despite substantially longer generations. This suggests that CoT does not primarily improve performance by prolonging direct image-token access throughout the reasoning trace, but by extending language-side computation over image-derived hidden-state information. We further show that CoT gains are constrained by perceptual readout. CoT helps when the queried visual attribute can be reliably read out by the model, but not when that readout is unreliable. A symbolic-attribute oracle shows that CoT can improve counting once ground-truth attributes are supplied as text, while a single-object probe-vs-decode check shows that hard attributes can be linearly recoverable from hidden states yet difficult for the model itself to output. Together, these analyses place the bottleneck at readout rather than counting.

</details>


### [185] [Human-AI Agent Interaction as a Neuroplastic Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12823)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Asanga Gunaratna,Ravi Mukkamala,Nihal Siriwardanagea,Gihan Siriwardanagea,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Sachin Shetty,Christopher K. Rhea,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Amin Hass,Shaifali Kaushik,Wathsala Herath,Preston Samuel,Anita H. Clayton,Atmaram Yarlagadd*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出日常AI交互（如对话、编程、图像生成）中的迭代循环构成了高频率的神经可塑性训练环境：当结果令人失望时，不耐烦、完美主义、挫败感等反应模式反复被激发，在突触可塑性机制下，未经干预的循环会通过长时程增强强化这些神经通路。作者提出利用同一环境产生相反效果：在反应模式被激活但尚未完成的"调节间隙"中，进行元认知观察（而非习惯性重新提示），通过长时程抑制削弱相关神经通路。框架包含三层观察和两种应用模式（用户引导和代理辅助），以生成式图像提示为例展示其神经效应与行为表现的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示日常AI交互中未被意识到的神经可塑性训练环境——用户在与AI迭代交互时，失望引发的负面反应模式在突触可塑性机制下被被动强化，这是一个未被充分认识的问题。

Method: 提出基于神经可塑性的干预框架：在条件性反应模式被激活但尚未完成表出的"调节间隙"中，进行背后观察（元认知监控），利用长时程抑制替代长时程增强，从而弱化而非强化负面神经通路。框架包括三层观察（对反应、对渴望、对觉察本身）和两种应用模式（用户引导和代理辅助）。

Result: 以生成式图像提示为例，展示了同一个令人沮丧的交互会话在行为层面几乎相同（是否进行观察），但在神经效应层面完全相反：未经观察的交互强化负面通路，经过观察的交互则弱化这些通路。

Conclusion: 日常AI代理交互不仅是工具使用过程，更是神经可塑性训练环境；通过有意识的元认知观察，可以将被动强化负面模式的循环转化为主动弱化这些模式的机会，且这一干预不需要改变现有工具。

Abstract: Interaction with AI agents has become one of the most frequent activities of everyday digital life. Whether conversing with an assistant, working with a coding copilot, or generating images, the interaction follows a common iterative loop: a request is issued, a result returned, appraised, and the request revised. We observe that this loop is a high-frequency stream of contact events -- moments at which a result meets a person and a conditioned response may fire before deliberate appraisal -- making everyday agent interaction an unrecognised neuroplastic training environment. When a result disappoints, reactive patterns of impatience, perfectionism, frustration, and self-criticism are repeatedly evoked, and under activity-dependent synaptic plasticity each uninterrupted cycle deepens the underlying pathway through long-term potentiation. Ordinary agent use may thus quietly strengthen the very patterns it provokes. We propose that the same training environment can be engaged to the opposite effect. Treating conditioned reactive patterns as physical neurone paths -- activated through a pre-cognitive feeling tone that opens a brief regulatory gap -- we develop a framework in which, at that gap, in place of the reactive re-prompt, a person performs behind-the-scenes observation: watching the neural process operate so the cascade does not complete and long-term depression weakens the path rather than potentiation strengthening it. We characterise this practice through three layers of observation and two modes of application: a user-guided mode requiring no change to existing tools, and an agent-assisted mode in which an ordinary agent is lightly configured to support observation at the gap. We illustrate the framework through generative image prompting, showing how a single frustrating session is behaviourally nearly identical whether or not it is observed, yet neurologically opposite.

</details>


### [186] [Solution of the Hempel's statistical ambiguity problem and Causal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12826)
*Evgenii Vityaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入因果规则和语义概率推理程序，定义了最大特异因果关系(MSCRs)，并证明了由此推导的预测是一致的，从而解决了Hempel的统计歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hempel提出的归纳统计推理中的统计歧义问题，即从统计定律推导出矛盾预测的长期难题。

Method: 基于Cartwright的因果定义引入因果规则概念，设计了一种语义概率推理程序，通过增量式精炼因果规则纳入所有统计相关信息，生成最大特异因果关系(MSCRs)。

Result: 证明了从MSCRs推导的预测是一致的（定理1），从而解决了统计歧义问题。

Conclusion: 该语义概率推理程序提供了一个可用于因果AI和因果机器学习的概率因果学习系统，并与不变特征学习、不变因果预测等概念相关联。

Abstract: This paper addresses Carl Hempel's longstanding problem of statistical ambiguity in inductive-statistical inference, in which contradictory predictions are derived from statistical laws. To avoid such predictions, Carl Hempel proposed the Requirement of Maximal Specificity (RMS) for the statistical laws used in the inference. An analysis of the RMS refinements made by Wesley Salmon, Alberto Coffa, and James Fetzer led to the following definition of maximally specific statistical laws: "the lawlike premises of an adequate explanation must specify all and only those properties whose presence or absence made a difference to the occurrence of its explanandum-phenomenon." However, there was no proof of a solution to the statistical ambiguity problem based on this definition. We use Nancy Cartwright's definition of causes that raise probabilities across background contexts, and then introduce the concept of Causal Rules. Then we define a special semantic probabilistic inference procedure that incrementally refines these causal rules by incorporating all statistically relevant information. This procedure yields Maximally Specific Causal Relationships (MSCRs), for which we prove (Theorem 1) that predictions derived from them are consistent. This resolves the statistical ambiguity problem. The semantic probabilistic inference procedure provides a probabilistic causal learning system, which may be used in such new areas as Causal AI and Causal Machine Learning. They fundamentally explore causal inference as a tool for understanding cause-and-effect relationships within complex systems. Properties similar to RMS remain under discussion. Several notions related to RMS are considered: invariant feature learning, invariant causal prediction, and spurious association.

</details>


### [187] [A Multi-Agent System for Autonomous, Fine-Tuning-Free Clinical Symptom Detection: Development and Validation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12886)
*Cameron Cagan,Pedram Fard,Jiazi Tian,Jingya Cheng,Shawn N. Murphy,Hossein Estiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythia是一个多智能体系统，能够在本地开源模型上自主编写和优化临床笔记中的症状提取提示，无需人工提示工程或微调。在400份临床笔记的72个症状上，Pythia实现了平均灵敏度0.76和特异性0.95，而人工构建的lexicon分别为0.82和0.76。对于lexicon标记所有笔记为阳性的14个概念，Pythia通过要求现在时、患者归因的发现恢复了0.97的特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床笔记中包含大量症状和体征信息，但这些信息很少被结构化记录。现有的提取方法要么依赖上下文不敏感的规则产生误报，要么需要大量微调的有监督模型，效率低下且难以部署。因此需要一种无需人工干预、能在本地基础设施上高效运行的自动化症状提取方法。

Method: 提出Pythia多智能体系统，在本地开源模型上自主编写和优化提取提示。系统利用开发集的敏感性和特异性选择提示，无需手动提示工程或微调。实验在387名患者的400份临床笔记中，针对72个症状和体征进行比较。每个概念独立划分开发集（n=300）和验证集（n=100）。

Result: Pythia平均灵敏度0.76，特异性0.95；lexicon分别为0.82和0.76。在62个可直接比较的概念中，Pythia在20个概念上匹配或超过lexicon。对于lexicon将所有笔记标记为阳性的14个概念，Pythia通过要求现在时、患者归因的发现实现特异性0.97。特异性从开发集到验证集迁移良好，但灵敏度在患病率低于5%时迁移减弱，低于2%时平均差距达0.25。BERT分类器在相同开发集上平均灵敏度仅0.23，在低于约5%患病率的概念上灵敏度降为零。

Conclusion: 自主的、无需微调的提示优化方法能够生成有效的症状提取提示，从开发集到验证集具有良好的泛化能力，且可部署在本地基础设施上，在保持高特异性的同时避免了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Clinical notes contain many of the signs and symptoms that bring patients to care, yet this information rarely reaches structured fields. Existing extraction approaches either rely on context-insensitive rules that generate false positives or on supervised models that require substantial fine-tuning. We present Pythia, a multi-agent system that autonomously writes and optimizes extraction prompts for clinical concepts without manual prompt engineering or fine-tuning. Running on a locally hosted open-weights model, Pythia keeps clinical notes on local infrastructure and selects prompts using development-set sensitivity and specificity. We compared Pythia with a curated lexicon across 72 signs and symptoms from 400 clinical notes representing 387 patients. Development (n=300) and validation (n=100) sets were partitioned independently for each concept. Pythia achieved mean sensitivity of 0.76 and specificity of 0.95, compared with 0.82 and 0.76 for the lexicon, and matched or exceeded the lexicon on both metrics for 20 of 62 directly comparable concepts. For 14 concepts where the lexicon labeled every note positive, Pythia recovered mean specificity of 0.97 by requiring a present-tense, patient-attributed finding rather than any textual mention of a term. Specificity transferred from development to validation with minimal degradation across prevalences, whereas sensitivity transfer weakened below 5% prevalence, reaching a mean gap of 0.25 below 2% prevalence. A BERT classifier fine-tuned per concept on the same development set achieved mean sensitivity of 0.23 and collapsed to zero sensitivity for concepts below roughly 5% prevalence. These findings suggest that autonomous, fine-tuning-free prompt optimization can produce symptom extraction prompts that generalize effectively from development to validation while remaining deployable on local infrastructure.

</details>


### [188] [MemOps: Benchmarking Lifecycle Memory Operations in Long-Horizon Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12893)
*Xixuan Hao,Zeyu Zhang,Zehao Lin,Yihang Sun,Ziliang Guo,Xichong Zhang,Yuxuan Liang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemOps基准，将对话记忆重新定义为一系列生命周期操作（记忆、遗忘、更新、反思及其组合），通过结构化轨迹和操作级探针进行细粒度评估，揭示现有系统在最终答案准确性之外的各种记忆失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆基准仅通过下游问答评估最终答案的正确性，这种黑箱式评估混淆了记忆失败的异质原因（如遗漏事实引入、操作绑定错误目标、依赖过期值等），无法准确诊断系统在动态长期交互中的记忆问题。

Method: MemOps将对话记忆形式化为生命周期操作的序列，每个记忆事件用结构化轨迹（触发条件、目标、范围、状态转换、支持证据）表示；通过可控生成流水线将操作嵌入长任务导向对话中，生成金标准操作轨迹和六类操作级探针，在相邻证据和长上下文两种设置下评估。

Result: 跨长上下文、检索增强、参数化和管理记忆系统的实验表明，MemOps能揭示最终答案准确性所掩盖的失败模式；例如会话级检索优于轮次级检索，长上下文模型在重建有序记忆状态轨迹方面表现明显薄弱。

Conclusion: 长时记忆评估应从最终答案评分转向可解释的操作级诊断，MemOps提供了这一方向的基准框架。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a foundational capability for LLM-based agents that accompany users across extended, multi-session interactions. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate such memory almost exclusively through downstream question answering, scoring only the correctness of a final answer. This black-box formulation conflates the heterogeneous causes of memory failure, such as missing the introduction of a relevant fact, binding an operation to the wrong target, or relying on stale values after a correction. As a result, it can credit correct answers despite their reliance on inconsistent or unsafe memory states. In this paper, we argue that, in dynamic long-horizon interactions, memory is not a static collection of facts but a lifecycle of explicit operations, including remembering, forgetting, updating, reflecting, and their compositions. We introduce MemOps, a benchmark that reformulates conversational memory as a sequence of lifecycle operations and represents each memory event with a structured trace specifying its trigger, target, scope, state transition, and supporting evidence. A controllable generation pipeline embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and produces gold operation traces together with six categories of operation-level probes, evaluated under both adjacent-evidence and long-context settings. Across long-context, retrieval-based, parametric and managed-memory systems, MemOps disentangles failure modes that final-answer accuracy alone conceals, revealing that current systems remain far from uniformly reliable. For instance, session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level retrieval, and long-context models remain notably weak at reconstructing ordered memory-state trajectories. These results move long-term memory evaluation from final-answer scoring toward interpretable, operation-level diagnosis.

</details>


### [189] [Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12924)
*Jonas Ehrhardt,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号知识与梯度引导的强化学习算法(KGRL)，通过在Datalog知识库中编码领域知识来剪枝不可行动作并约束参数空间，结合梯度参数优化，在PAMDP环境中显著提升了样本效率和回合回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作MDP(PAMDP)中现有强化学习算法使用单次估计确定参数，样本效率低下；同时大量显式但不完备的领域知识(规则、安全约束、专家启发式)未被充分利用来提升样本效率。

Method: 1) 将领域知识编码为Datalog知识库，在给定状态下推导可行动作与可行参数集；2) 利用知识库剪枝非可行动作并约束参数空间；3) 采用基于梯度的参数精化循环在训练和部署阶段估计最优参数；4) 通过记录轨迹中激活的规则提供局部程序性解释。

Result: KGRL在样本效率和回合回报两个指标上均优于PAMDPs的现有最先进强化学习基线。

Conclusion: KGRL通过神经符号方法有效融合领域知识引导探索与决策，提升了样本效率，同时提供了可解释的局部推理过程。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Reinforcement Learning in Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes (PAMDP), where each decision consists of a symbolic action and numerical parameters. In such settings Reinforcement Learning algorithms typically determine parameters with one-shot estimators, which makes their training sample inefficient. Though in most PAMDP environments explicit but incomplete knowledge (e.g., rules, safety constraints, or expert heuristics) is available, it is rarely directly used to increase the sample-efficiency of training Reinforcement Learning agents. We step into this gap and propose our novel Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning (KGRL) algorithm. KGRL uses domain knowledge in a Datalog knowledge base to derive the set of applicable actions and feasible parameters for a given state. This allows it to prune non-applicable actions from the decision-space and constrain the parameter spaces of the remaining actions. We then use a gradient-based parameter refinement loop to estimate the optimal parameters during training and deployment of the agent. By recording activated rules along the trajectory, KGRL additionally provides local procedural explanations on the pruning of actions and constraining of parameters. Overall, KGRL guides the agent's exploration and deployment toward feasible and constraint-aware decisions, while increasing sample efficiency during training. KGRL outperforms state-of-the-art RL baselines for PAMDPs in both, sample efficiency and episodic return.

</details>


### [190] [FormalAnalyticGeo: A Neural-Symbolic Based Framework for Multimodal Analytic Geometry Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12982)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FormalAnalyticGeo框架，通过形式语言CDL和SDF引擎自动生成解析几何多模态问题，构建了包含7K+验证样本的AnalyticGeo7K数据集，无需人工标注，生成问题的答案中位相对误差仅0.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在数学推理方面取得显著进展，但解析几何领域因标注样本稀缺而研究不足。现有图解生成方法无法处理解析几何中约束驱动的布局和圆锥曲线的精确渲染。

Method: 设计CDL（条件描述语言）作为形式中间表示，桥接自由形式的问题文本与基于符号距离场引擎的精确图解渲染。框架顺序使用四个专用LLM组件：Generator生成多样问题、Formalizer将问题转为CDL、Measurer通过视觉测量提取真实答案、Quality Verifier在三阶段检查输出质量，质量反馈驱动自动重试形成闭环。

Result: 生成AnalyticGeo7K数据集（7K+验证问题），中位相对误差0.70%，82.3%的答案在精确符号解5%误差范围内。

Conclusion: FormalAnalyticGeo实现了无需人工标注的解析几何多模态问题自动生成，验证了形式语言结合SDF渲染在几何精确性上的有效性，框架和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Math reasoning has achieved significant progress with the rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), however analytic geometry remains largely underexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated samples. Existing diagram generation approaches struggle with analytic geometry: template methods cannot handle constraint-driven layouts, and generative models lack the geometric precision to render annotated conic curves correctly. We present FormalAnalyticGeo, a scalable framework for fully automatic generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems. Leveraging the rigor of formal languages, we design the framework around CDL (Condition Description Language), a formal intermediate representation that bridges free-form problem text with precise diagram rendering via a Signed Distance Field (SDF) engine. The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground truth.Experiments show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [191] [Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12985)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反事实报告坐标（CRC）夹持的方法，通过因果干预实现语言模型对非证据性激励压力的抗性，同时保持对真实证据的响应，在贝叶斯证人基准上达到联合1.00的因果认证指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型在非证据性激励压力下（如用户自信、过度确定）会系统性误报，即使内部信念未变。这暴露了模型缺乏内部激励兼容性（internal IC），需要一种机制让模型报告仅响应真实证据而免受非证据性影响。

Method: (i) 通过交换干预（interchange interventions）而非探针准确性，因果识别出报告坐标中低秩、近正交且可独立控制的答案/置信度/谨慎性维度；(ii) 引入无需训练的反事实报告坐标（CRC）夹持，参考模型在反事实激励中性化上下文下的自身报告，两遍夹持实现联合认证；同时探索全局解码、参数化权衡、输出级微调及可部署单遍编译方案。

Result: 双遍夹持在证人基准上实现resist和update联合1.00（Wilson 95% CI [0.99, 1.00]）；全局解码与引导显示单参数权衡；输出级微调仅在同时枚举两个目标时匹配；仅resist训练丢失证据响应；可部署单遍编译有损（0.73/0.97）；机制跨三个模型家族复现并迁移至自然谄媚基准（SycophancyEval）。

Conclusion: 贡献在于接口与认证方法：将激活层反事实激励不变性作为内部激励兼容性的结构化基元，而非直接提供可部署解决方案。

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [192] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了规划评估器可能奖励策略性计划变得不够明确的问题，提出了一种阶段性期望值评分器，通过理论分析、实验验证和PCSC/GATE机制，揭示了评分系统对省略关键工作的激励，并提供了检测与消除此类省略的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划评估器可能因忽略显式细节而给予更高分数，这种评分缺陷会激励模型生成看似高分但实际省略必要工作的策略，需要对此类失败模式进行系统研究并提出解决方案。

Method: 采用阶段性期望值评分器（staged expected-value scorer）分析LLM生成的创业路径；提出命题1描述删除内部转移的分数变化公式；通过冻结的26条路线队列进行57次可容许删除验证；构建分数优化器进行结构重组实验；提出GATE机制和PCSC（Post-hoc Omission Splice Detection and Correction）方法。

Result: 所有57次可容许删除均匹配分析恒等式和阈值符号；每条路线至少有一个分数提升的删除；21/26条路线找到基线更优结构；GATE拒绝26/26条静默路线的分数释放且0/26次诚实挂起；47/54次后续修订修复为覆盖结构；严格覆盖改进从1/26提升至13/26；delta索引成本下限使beat-honest路线从6/6降至3/6，fundability-by-silence从5/6降至0/6。

Conclusion: 如果计划仅因省略必要工作而获得更高分数，则计划并未真正改进，评分系统创造了省略激励。PCSC可检测并消除事后省略拼接，GATE在协作环境中作为确定性搜索塑造约束而非仅事后过滤器，但无法验证任意LLM生成策略的语义完整性或真实质量。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [193] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在高度不确定性环境（如含有随机和隐藏信息的对抗性棋盘游戏）中的应用，提出了两项动态资源分配增强策略：动态确定化次数（动态调整确定化树的数量）和动态模拟分配（非均匀分配模拟预算）。在Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三款桌游上的实验表明，特定配置在迭代和时间限制下显著提升了算法强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCTS变体在不确定性高的环境中（如包含随机性和隐藏信息的对抗性桌游）性能有限，传统的静态资源分配方式无法根据搜索过程动态调整，导致计算资源利用效率不足。

Method: 基于Ensemble Determinization MCTS框架，提出两项动态资源分配增强：（1）动态确定化次数——根据搜索行为动态增减少数确定化树的数量；（2）动态模拟分配——在确定化树之间非均匀分配模拟预算，通过模拟间决策选择潜在知识增益最大的树。

Result: 在Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三款桌游上，分别在迭代和时间两种限制设置下进行测试，特定配置的增强算法相比基线取得了统计学上显著的强度提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态资源分配机制能够有效提升Ensemble Determinization MCTS在不完全信息博弈中的表现，为高不确定性环境下的搜索算法优化提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [194] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffusionGemma，一个26B参数混合专家模型，通过离散扩散语言模型以约8个并行去噪步骤转录语音，在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%词错误率，仅训练了42M参数（占骨干网络的0.16%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动语音识别主要由自回归解码器主导，每次只生成一个token。作者探究能否使用离散扩散语言模型替代自回归解码器，通过少量并行去噪步骤同时优化整个转录文本，从而提高解码效率。

Method: 使用DiffusionGemma（26B MoE模型）作为骨干，采用均匀随机token离散扩散（而非吸收掩码方案）；冻结Whisper编码器提取声学特征，轻量投影器映射到模型嵌入空间；低秩适配器使冻结骨干网络适应新模态；仅训练约42M参数（0.16%）。针对音频无法有效接地的问题，提出通过冻结输出头应用CTC损失来打破梯度死锁。

Result: 在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%词错误率；无论话语长度如何，约8个并行步骤即可完成转录；单个适配器在六种语言上训练，在英语、印地语和普通话上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 离散扩散语言模型可以用于语音转录，以接近恒定的并行步骤高效解码；标准的训练目标无法有效将音频与模型对齐，需要引入CTC损失来解决问题。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality.
  About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [195] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务感知的执行范围估计方法E3，让LLM agent在自动化任务前先评估任务难度和所需信息，仅执行最小必要路径，从而实现与最强基线持平的100%成功率的同时降低85%成本、91%token消耗和92%检查文件数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent在自动化多步骤工程工作流时，往往采用"最大上下文优先"策略——重复读取已看过的文件和依赖——将简单的一行编辑变成小型代码库审计。它们缺乏"任务感知的执行范围估计"能力，即在投入计算预算之前判断任务难度、真正需要的信息以及最短可靠路径。

Method: 形式化定义了"最小充分执行"和"Agent认知冗余比（ACRR）"；提出E3框架：(1) Estimate：agent估计初始操作点，(2) Execute：执行最小可行路径，(3) Expand：仅在验证失败时扩展范围。在MSE-Bench（121个编辑的确定性基准）和LLM-Case（基于gpt-4o的真实开源库编辑）两个设置下进行评估。

Result: 在MSE-Bench上，E3与最强基线达到100%成功率，同时成本降低85%、token减少91%、检查文件减少92%；比强自适应检索基线高出16%；在LLM-Case真实模型测试中，E3是最精简、最快的策略，任务成功率相当，唯一不足是provider的速率限制而非编辑错误。

Conclusion: 任务感知的执行是迈向"工程扎根AI（EGAI）"的一步——agent的工作量应锚定在任务的工程现实中。该框架作为一个受控的执行冗余探针，而非对任何已部署agent的测量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Cyclic codes over a commutative non-unitary ring of order 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11965)
*Marvin Olavides,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了环I₂上的循环码，通过商码和挠码建立了与二元准循环码的联系，并完成了长度≤7的循环码分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I₂是非幺交换环分类中出现的4阶环，其上编码理论研究较少，探索其循环码结构与性质具有理论意义。

Method: 利用剩余码和挠码（基于F₂）刻画I₂上的循环码；引入扭曲与非扭曲循环码概念，通过扭曲映射与循环移位的兼容性条件刻画循环性；通过Gray映射建立与二元准循环码的联系；利用对偶性证明循环码的对偶仍然是循环码。

Result: Gray像是二元指数为2的准循环码；循环码的对偶依然循环；完成了长度≤7的置换不等价循环码分类及其结构性质刻画。

Conclusion: I₂上的循环码可以通过二元域上的商码和挠码系统研究，并与二元准循环码紧密关联，为小长度循环码提供了完整的分类结果。

Abstract: Let $I_2$ be the commutative non-unitary ring of order $4$ arising in the classification of Fine. In this paper, we investigate cyclic codes over $I_2$ through their associated residue and torsion codes over $\mathbb{F}_2$. We introduce the notions of twisted and untwisted cyclic codes and characterize cyclicity in terms of a compatibility condition involving the twist map and the cyclic shift. Connections between cyclic codes over $I_2$ and binary quasi-cyclic codes are established via Gray maps. In particular, we show that the Gray image of a cyclic code over $I_2$ is a binary quasi-cyclic code of index $2$. We also study duality properties of cyclic codes over $I_2$ and prove that the dual of a cyclic code is again cyclic. Finally, we classify permutation inequivalent cyclic codes over $I_2$ for lengths $n \le 7$ and determine various structural properties of these codes.

</details>


### [197] [Generalized Segmented GRAND for Guesswork Reduction in Turbo Product Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12147)
*Lukas Rapp,Jiewei Feng,Muriel Médard,Ken R. Duffy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出GSegGRAND，一种泛化的分段GRAND算法，通过新型校验矩阵结构和变换方法，利用多达log2(n)个约束条件，相比Segmented GRAND减少75%猜题工作。结合推导的软输出方程应用于Turbo乘积码译码，可减少88%猜题工作，适用于未来低延迟通信系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Segmented GRAND虽能通过约束猜测降低猜题工作量，但其依赖特定的校验矩阵结构，限制了可使用的约束数量及适用码类，需要更通用的方法以充分利用约束猜测的优势。

Method: 提出GSegGRAND，基于新型奇偶校验矩阵结构和码字变换方法，将广泛类型码字映射到该结构上，高效融入多达log2(n)个约束；并扩展SOGRAND框架推导GSegGRAND的软输出方程。

Result: 相比Segmented GRAND，GSegGRAND在硬判决下减少75%猜题工作；结合软输出应用于Turbo乘积码译码，可减少高达88%猜题工作。

Conclusion: GSegGRAND克服了Segmented GRAND的局限性，显著降低猜题工作量，是未来低延迟通信系统中具有前景的译码候选方案。

Abstract: Guessing random additive noise decoding (GRAND) can efficiently decode any moderately redundant code with near maximum likelihood (ML) performance via noise effect guessing. For binary linear codes, Rowshan and Yuan's Segmented GRAND was the first to show that constrained guessing can reduce guesswork. Although powerful, their approach requires a specific parity-check matrix structure that limits the number of constraints that can be exploited as well as the class of applicable codes. Here we introduce GSegGRAND, a generalization of Segmented GRAND that circumvents its limitations. Built on a novel parity check structure and a transformation that maps codes into this structure, GSegGRAND efficiently incorporates up to log2(n) constraints for a wide range of codes, reducing guesswork by an additional 75% over Segmented GRAND. To leverage that advantage for soft-output decoding, we derive an accurate soft-output (SO) equation for GSegGRAND by extending soft-output GRAND (SOGRAND) to incorporate constrained guessing. Applying this SO to turbo product decoding, GSegGRAND achieves up to 88% guesswork reduction, making it a promising candidate for low-latency decoding in future communication systems.

</details>


### [198] [Auxiliary Nodes for BP Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12187)
*Daniel Tandler,Paul Bezner,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，通过在CSS量子LDPC码的解码图中引入辅助变量节点（AVN）和辅助校验节点（ACN），改善短环和简并性等影响置信传播（BP）解码性能的问题，并展示了该框架的统一解释能力及在电路级噪声下的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年提出的CSS QLDPC码虽有稀疏解码图支持低复杂度的基于综合征的BP解码，但其构造常导致短环和简并性等损害BP性能的问题，亟需一种通用方法来解决这些缺陷。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，在CSS码的解码图中引入辅助变量节点（AVN）和辅助校验节点（ACN），兼容标准稳定子测量框架；将4-环移除和子码集成解码等现有技术统一解释为该框架的特例，并进一步提出基于图衍生子码的集成解码器。

Result: 4-环移除的增益强烈依赖于BP迭代次数和校验节点消息缩放；所提出的图衍生子码集成解码器在电路级噪声下，相较于对应4-环无环解码图上的BP解码，显著降低了每轮逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 该框架为解码图设计提供了额外自由度，能有效改善BP解码性能，且所提子码集成解码器在电路级噪声下表现优异。

Abstract: Many recently proposed Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes have sparse decoding graphs, enabling syndrome-based belief propagation (BP) decoding at low complexity. Their construction, however, often results in properties that impair BP performance, such as short cycles and degeneracy. In this work, we propose a general framework for introducing auxiliary variable nodes (AVNs) and auxiliary check nodes (ACNs) into the decoding graph of CSS codes, compatible with the standard stabilizer measurement framework. This provides an additional degree of freedom in the design of the decoding graph itself and can be used to tackle the aforementioned shortcomings. We show that recently proposed techniques, 4-cycle removal and subcode ensemble decoding, can be interpreted as instances of this framework. For 4-cycle removal, we find that the gains depend strongly on the BP iteration count and check-node message scaling. Building on this framework, we further propose a graph-derived subcode ensemble decoder and demonstrate under circuit-level noise that it substantially reduces the per-round logical error rate compared with BP on the corresponding 4-cycle-free decoding graph.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum Codes from $r$-Nearly Self-Orthogonal Linear Codes via Jordan Canonical Form over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12242)
*Liangdong Lu,Ruipan Yang,Yang Liu,Qiang Fu,Guanmin Guo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Jordan标准形的框架，利用任意经典线性码（无需满足自正交条件）构建q元量子稳定子码。通过测量Hermitian自正交性障碍的秩r，将经典码转化为自正交码，并保证距离不减，从而生成参数为[[n+r, 2k-n+r, ≥d]]_q的量子码，在Grassl表格中改进了多项记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子稳定子码的构造要求经典线性码满足对偶包含条件（自正交性），这一条件限制了可用的经典码范围。本文旨在突破这一限制，实现从任意经典线性码构造量子码，并探索新的量子码参数。

Method: 定义了Hermitian自正交性障碍的秩r = rank(HH†)，表征经典码偏离自正交性的程度。利用Jordan分解A = P J_A P⁻¹，通过沿Jordan基W = P⁻¹进行秩一摄动，系统降低Hermitian内积矩阵A=HH†的秩，构造出一个显式的Hermitian自正交码C_so = [n+r, n-k]_{q²}，并给出了充分的距离保持准则。

Result: 得到量子码参数为[[n+r, 2k-n+r, ≥d]]_q。应用该构造于经典码，在Grassl量子码表格中产生多项改进或补充的记录量子码。

Conclusion: 基于Jordan标准形的框架成功突破了经典码必须自正交的限制，为量子码构造提供了新的系统性方法，并取得了实际参数改进。

Abstract: We introduce a Jordan-canonical-form framework for constructing $q$-ary quantum stabilizer codes from arbitrary classical linear codes over $\F_{q^2}$. The framework does not require the classical linear code $\mathcal{C}$ to satisfy the dual-containing condition (i.e., self-orthogonality). Given a classical code $\mathcal{C}=[n,k,d]_{q^2}$ with parity-check matrix $H$, we measure the obstruction to Hermitian self-orthogonality by the rank $r=(n-k)-\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}\cap \mathcal{C})$. The ingredient code $\mathcal{C}$ is $r$-nearly dual containing, or, equivalently, $\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}$ is $r$-nearly self-orthogonal, by which we mean that $r=\Rank(HH^{\dagger})=\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h})-\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}\cap \mathcal{C})$. By systematically reducing the rank of the Hermitian inner-product matrix $A=HH^{\dagger}$ through rank-one perturbations along the Jordan basis $W=P^{-1}$ of the decomposition $A=PJ_AP^{-1}$, we construct an explicit Hermitian self-orthogonal code $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{so}}=[n+r,n-k]_{q^2}$. A sufficient distance-preservation criterion guarantees that the resulting $q$-ary quantum code has parameters $[[n+r,2k-n+r,\geq d]]_q$. Applying this construction to classical codes produces several record quantum codes that improve or supplement the best-known parameters in Grassl's tables.

</details>


### [200] [Metasurface Antenna-Enabled LEO Satellite Constellation Communications: Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12328)
*Wenfei Yao,Xiaoming Chen,Qi Wang,Qiao Qi,Ming Ying*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低地球轨道卫星星座在频谱效率和硬件复杂度方面的瓶颈，提出了一种基于超表面天线的卫星架构。通过超表面天线在波域实现高精度波束赋形，并设计了联合优化馈电天线调度与超表面无源波束赋形的交替优化算法（结合MCMF与WMMSE-SDR方法），有效提升了加权和速率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星星座面临频谱效率瓶颈和星载硬件复杂度高的挑战，现有技术难以满足多用户干扰抑制和系统性能提升的需求。

Method: 提出超表面天线（MA）架构，在波域实现高精度波束赋形；构建加权和速率（WSR）最大化问题，联合优化馈电天线调度与超表面无源波束赋形；采用交替优化（AO）算法，其中调度部分采用多项式时间的最小成本最大流（MCMF）方法，波束赋形部分采用加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）结合半定松弛（SDR）技术。

Result: 仿真验证了所提算法在MA-enabled LEO卫星星座通信中的有效性，在加权和速率性能上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于超表面天线的架构与联合调度波束赋形算法能够有效克服LEO卫星星座的频谱效率和硬件复杂度瓶颈，为下一代卫星通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Next-generation low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations face critical bottlenecks in spectral efficiency and onboard hardware complexity. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces a novel architecture enabled by metasurface antennas (MAs) at the LEO satellites. In particular, MAs are metasurface-integrated feed antennas that perform high-precision beamforming directly in the wave domain, thereby effectively mitigating multi-user interference. Based on such an antenna architecture, a weighted sum rate (WSR) maximization problem is formulated by jointly optimizing the scheduling of feed antennas to terrestrial users (TUs) and the passive beamforming of the metasurface for system performance enhancement. To address this mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) challenge, an alternating optimization (AO)-based joint scheduling and beamforming algorithm is proposed. On the one hand, the proposed algorithm incorporates a polynomial-time minimum-cost maximum-flow (MCMF) method, which is dedicated to the optimal scheduling of feed antennas and TUs. On the other hand, it adopts a weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) method integrated with semidefinite relaxation (SDR) technique, which is tailored for metasurface beamforming design. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm for MA-enabled LEO satellite constellation communications.

</details>


### [201] [Robust Design of Integrated Sensing and Communication in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12337)
*Hezhen Yang,Xiaoming Chen,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星系统中感知与通信功能融合的需求，提出了一种鲁棒波束赋形设计算法，在信道相位不确定性条件下同时满足感知的均方误差要求和通信的信干噪比要求，并最小化总发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着卫星感知与通信需求的增长，有限的无线资源难以支撑多卫星系统，因此希望在低轨卫星系统中实现通感一体化（ISAC），使单颗卫星具备多功能性，从而节省频谱和轨道资源。

Method: 建立低轨卫星ISAC框架，卫星在同一频谱上同时感知多个目标并为多个通信用户服务；考虑星上能量有限，提出鲁棒波束赋形设计算法，在信道相位不确定性条件下最小化总发射功率，同时满足感知的MSE约束和通信的SINR约束。

Result: 理论分析证明了所提算法的有效性，仿真结果表明该算法优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒波束赋形算法能够在信道相位不确定性下有效实现低轨卫星系统的通感一体化，兼顾感知与通信性能并节省发射功率。

Abstract: With the growing demand for satellite sensing and communication, the limited wireless resources are difficult to support multiple satellite systems. Therefore, it is desired to investigate integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite systems to enable multi-functionality within a single satellite, thereby saving both spectrum and orbital resources. In this paper, a framework for ISAC in LEO satellite systems is established, where a satellite can simultaneously sense multiple targets and serve multiple communication users (CUs) over the same spectrum. Considering the limited onboard energy of satellite, a novel robust beamforming design algorithm is developed with the goal of minimizing total transmit power while satisfying the mean squared error (MSE) requirements for sensing and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) requirements for communication in presence of channel phase uncertainty which exacerbates the cross-functional interference. According to theoretical analysis, the proposed algorithm for ISAC in LEO satellite systems is effective. Moreover, extensive simulations confirm the superiority of the proposed algorithm over baselines.

</details>


### [202] [A Superposition Signaling Scheme for Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12549)
*Gökhan Yılmaz,Hamdi Joudeh,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM集成感知与通信系统的容量问题，将集成旁瓣电平作为输入代价，问题简化为具有功率和峰度约束的复高斯噪声信道容量问题。感知最优输入为恒模信号，通信最优输入为复高斯信号，容量最优输入为离散多环几何结构。提出叠加策略作为简单可调的传输方案，通过逐次解码获得精确速率表达式，数值结果表明该策略与容量最坏情况差距约0.043比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中，感知性能要求（如低旁瓣电平）会限制通信传输的波形设计，因此需要研究在感知约束下通信信道容量的理论极限。

Method: 将ISAC系统的优化问题转化为复高斯噪声信道下具有功率和峰度约束的容量问题；分析感知最优（恒模）和通信最优（复高斯）输入的特征；提出叠加两种极值输入的可调策略，并用逐次解码推导精确速率。

Result: 叠加策略与容量最坏情况差距仅为约0.043比特，是一种接近最优的简单实用传输方案。

Conclusion: 在感知约束下，叠加恒模信号和复高斯信号的策略能以极小代价逼近信道容量，为ISAC系统设计提供了理论依据和实用波形方案。

Abstract: We investigate the capacity of an integrated sensing and communication system operating with orthogonal frequency division multiplexing, where the integrated sidelobe level of the transmit signal is adopted as an input cost. The problem is reduced to the capacity of a complex additive white Gaussian noise channel under power and kurtosis constraints. The sensing-optimal and communication-optimal operating points are characterized by circularly symmetric constant-modulus and complex Gaussian inputs, respectively, while the capacity-achieving input in between has discrete amplitude support with a concentric multi-ring geometry. We propose the superposition of the two extremal inputs as a simple and tunable signaling strategy, whose rate admits an exact expression via successive decoding. Numerical results show that the proposed strategy attains a worst-case gap of approximately 0.043 bits to the capacity.

</details>


### [203] [FM-Receiver: A Foundation Model Enabled Unified Inner and Outer Neural Receiver Towards AI-Native Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12555)
*Tianyue Zheng,Chao Jiang,Linglong Dai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基础模型（FM）的统一神经接收机FM-Receiver，将外接收机和内接收机整合到单一AI原生框架中，通过分组纠错码Transformer实现符号级信道解码，并采用三阶段配置自适应预训练策略，在不同系统配置下均优于基线方法，且对未见过的频段和场景具有强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经接收机仅将深度学习应用于外接收机，而保留传统的信道解码用于内接收机，这阻碍了联合优化，难以构建高效且统一的AI原生接收机。

Method: 1) 提出分组纠错码Transformer（GECT），实现符号级信道解码，无缝集成内外接收机；2) 构建FM-Receiver，直接以接收信号作为FM输入，输出恢复的传输比特；3) 设计三阶段配置自适应预训练策略，提升对多样化系统配置和场景的泛化能力。

Result: 在不同系统配置下，FM-Receiver的性能优于基线方法，并对未见过的频段和场景展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: FM-Receiver通过基础模型实现了内外接收机的统一AI原生框架，在性能和泛化能力上均优于传统方案，为构建高效统一的AI原生无线接收机提供了新思路。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI) techniques, neural receivers, which apply AI to improve wireless receivers have been developed. However, most existing neural receivers apply deep learning only to the outer receiver while retaining conventional channel decoding for the inner receiver, which prevents joint optimization and makes it difficult to build efficient and unified AI-native receivers. To address this issue, we propose a foundation model (FM)-enabled unified neural receiver, FM-Receiver, that integrates the outer and inner receivers into a single AI-native framework, by leveraging the strong representation capability of FMs. Specifically, we introduce a grouped error correction code Transformer that performs symbol-level channel decoding, enabling seamless integration of the inner and outer receiver. Building on this, we illustrate the proposed FM-Receiver, that directly takes the received signals as input of FM and outputs the recovered transmitted bits. In addition, a three-stage configuration-adaptive pre-training strategy is designed to improve the generalization ability to diverse system configurations and scenarios. Extensive simulations show that the proposed FM-Receiver achieves better performance than baselines across different system configurations. It also demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to unseen frequency bands and scenarios.

</details>


### [204] [Switched-Feed Pinching-Antenna Systems for Wideband Terahertz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12646)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对太赫兹（THz）频段 pinching-antenna system (PASS) 中波导衰减不可忽略的问题，建立了统一宽带传播模型，提出了 Switched-Feed PASS (SF-PASS) 架构，在THz最佳工作点显著优于单馈PASS，且与大规模天线阵列性能相当但硬件成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PASS分析认为波导内衰减在低频和毫米波波段可忽略，但该假设在THz频段不成立，因为THz频段可实现波导损耗远大于低频段。

Method: 建立了统一宽带THz-PASS传播模型，集成波导内衰减、大气吸收、分子再辐射噪声和波束斜视；推导了频带平均相干因子、满足带边SINR均衡条件的簇中心放置方案、放置反转阈值等闭式结果；提出了SF-PASS架构，中心射频开关在多波导段间路由信号，并给出插入损耗收益阈值。

Result: 在最佳PASS兼容THz工作点，SF-PASS频谱效率显著优于单馈PASS，且与大规模天线阵列性能相当但硬件成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: SF-PASS架构有效解决了THz频段波导衰减问题，是PASS在THz频段实际部署的可行方案。

Abstract: The pinching-antenna system (PASS) uses dielectric particles along a low-loss waveguide as reconfigurable passive radiators. Existing analyses conclude that the in-waveguide attenuation is negligible at low frequencies and millimeter wave bands; we show this fails at terahertz (THz), where realizable waveguide losses are dramatically larger. We develop a unified wideband THz-PASS propagation model integrating in-waveguide attenuation, atmospheric absorption, molecular re-radiation noise, and beam squint. Closed-form results follow: a band-averaged coherence factor; a cluster-center placement satisfying a band-edge SINR equalization condition; an associated placement-inversion threshold; and a proposed \emph{Switched-Feed PASS} (SF-PASS) architecture in which a centrally located radio-frequency switch routes the signal among multiple waveguide segments, with a closed-form insertion-loss payoff threshold. Numerical evaluation at the best PASS-compatible THz operating point shows that SF-PASS substantially outperforms single-feed PASS in spectral efficiency and is competitive with a large-scale antenna array at much lower hardware costs.

</details>


### [205] [Pinching-Antenna-Assisted Terahertz Communications: Modeling and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12695)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个太赫兹频段夹捏天线系统（THz-PASS）分析框架，集成了波导内传播衰减、倏逝波耦合及分子吸收再辐射噪声等THz特有效应，通过与相控阵对比，证明其在受限线性部署场景中通过近距利用获得频谱效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PASS研究主要集中在毫米波频段，扩展到太赫兹频段时会出现新的电磁现象，现有建模假设不再适用，因此需要建立专门的THz-PASS理论分析框架。

Method: 1. 建立集成式分析框架，包含波导内传播衰减、基于耦合模理论的倏逝波耦合、THz特有分子吸收及再辐射噪声；2. 在相同传播场景下与常规相控阵进行基准对比评估。

Result: THz-PASS通过近距利用在频谱效率上实现了有效增益，特别适用于受限和线性部署拓扑结构。

Conclusion: THz-PASS在受限线性部署场景下相比传统相控阵具有优势，是一种有前景的太赫兹灵活天线架构。

Abstract: Pinching antenna systems (PASS) employing dielectric waveguides have recently emerged as a promising flexible antenna architecture for high-frequency wireless communications. While prior work has focused primarily on millimeter-wave regimes, extending PASS to the terahertz (THz) band introduces distinct electromagnetic phenomena that invalidate conventional modeling assumptions. This paper develops the first analytical framework for THz-PASS that integrates in-waveguide propagation attenuation, evanescent coupling via coupled-mode theory, and THz-specific free-space effects including molecular absorption and its re-radiation noise. Using this model, we benchmark THz-PASS against conventional phased arrays under identical propagation scenarios. Our comparative evaluation reveals that THz-PASS achieves effective gains in spectral efficiency through proximity exploitation, making it particularly well-suited for confined and linear deployment topologies.

</details>


### [206] [Superimposed Transmission for Cooperative Cellular and Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12709)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于协作蜂窝和无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统的叠加传输策略，通过将近用户和远用户分类，基站在分布式接入点信号上叠加每个近用户的额外数据符号，并采用连续干扰消除解码，在保证小区边缘公平性的同时实现了最高峰值频谱效率，系统容量优于现有网络配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作蜂窝和无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统在提升系统容量的同时，难以兼顾小区边缘用户的公平性和频谱效率的峰值性能。

Method: 将用户分为近用户和远用户两类，基站在分布式接入点传输的信号上叠加每个近用户的额外数据符号，近用户接收端采用连续干扰消除（SIC）技术依次解码两个符号。

Result: 所提策略实现了最高的峰值频谱效率，同时保持了小区边缘的公平性，系统容量优于所有现有网络配置。

Conclusion: 叠加传输策略能够有效平衡频谱效率与公平性，为协作蜂窝和无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统提供了一种性能更优的网络配置方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a superimposed transmission strategy for cooperative cellular and cell-free massive MIMO systems. By classifying users into near and far, the base station transmits an additional data symbol for each near user, superimposed on the signals from distributed access points. Successive interference cancellation is employed at near-user receivers to decode both symbols. The proposed strategy achieves the highest peak spectral efficiency while maintaining fairness at the cell edge, thereby outperforming all the existing network configurations in system capacity.

</details>


### [207] [A Survey on Code Equivalence: The State-of-the-Art and Open Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12870)
*Anna-Lena Horlemann,Abhinaba Mazumder,Michael Schaller,Violetta Weger*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了代码等价问题及其变体，阐述了现有成果、不同问题形式之间的关系、算法技术和硬度假设，系统回顾了已知攻击及其有效参数范围和局限性，并指出了若干开放问题和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理代码等价问题的研究现状，理清不同问题形式、算法技术与硬度假设之间的关系，明确当前攻击手段的有效范围及局限性，并指出开放问题以指导未来研究方向。

Method: 采用综合性文献综述方法，对代码等价问题及其变体进行系统分类与对比分析，梳理不同问题形式之间的关系，归纳算法技术，整理已知攻击方法及其参数有效性范围。

Result: 系统性地呈现了代码等价问题的研究全貌，包括各类变体之间的关联、不同算法技术的适用场景、已知攻击方法及其有效参数区间和局限性。

Conclusion: 当前对代码等价问题的理解仍需深化，尤其是在开放问题和未充分探索的攻击场景方面，未来工作需要进一步揭示该问题的硬度本质。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a comprehensive survey of the code equivalence problem and its variants. We explain the existing results, highlighting the relationships between different problem formulations, algorithmic techniques, and hardness assumptions. In addition, we systematically review known attacks, identify the parameter regimes in which they are effective, and discuss their limitations. Lastly, we outline several open problems and research directions, with the aim of clarifying the current landscape and guiding future work toward a deeper understanding of the hardness of code equivalence.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Cycles in Liquid Democracy: A Game-Theoretic Justification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12610)
*Markus Brill,Rachael Colley,Anne-Marie George,Grzegorz Lisowski,Georgios Papasotiropoulos,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对液态民主中委托循环这一争议现象，提出了一种包含不确定性下战略行为的模型，从理论和实验两方面证明委托循环的必然性与合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献批评液态民主中的委托循环会导致投票权失效，但实践者认为循环是有意形成的且无问题。理论与现实存在脱节，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 建立考虑不确定性下战略行为的数学模型，研究纳什均衡的存在性、结构与质量，并通过最佳响应动力学进行计算实验。

Result: 揭示委托循环在均衡中自然会涌现，计算实验也支持这一结论。

Conclusion: 委托循环并非问题，而是液态民主中参与者战略行为的自然结果，理论模型应与实践观察一致。

Abstract: A common criticism of liquid democracy within the relevant academic literature is that delegation cycles can occur, seemingly resulting in unused voting power. Yet, practitioners argue that delegation cycles are not only unproblematic but are even formed intentionally by participants. To bring theory closer to reality, we introduce a model that captures this strategic behavior under uncertainty. We study the existence, structure and quality of Nash equilibria, revealing that delegation cycles naturally emerge. To complement these findings, we perform computational experiments using best-response dynamics.

</details>


### [209] [Quiz Show Games: Searching with Bimodal Hiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12867)
*Mingshi Yao,Thomas Lidbetter,Melike Baykal-Gürsoy*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个问答游戏节目中的博弈论问题：参赛者需以最优顺序回答问题以最大化期望收益，而节目主持人则可以通过分配固定奖金预算来对抗参赛者的策略。文章分析了三种不同的奖金分配方式变体，给出了完整或部分的均衡解，并将该问题与国家安全中的侦察任务和核浓缩证据检查等操作搜索问题联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的经典版本（Kadane, 1969）已有简单的基于索引的最优解，但实际场景中（如国家安全侦察任务、核浓缩证据检查以及调度问题）存在一个博弈方（主持人/对手）可以主动分配预算来对抗决策者的策略，因此需要研究博弈论版本。

Method: 研究了三种奖金分配方式的博弈变体：第一种给出了完全闭式解（均衡策略和博弈价值）；第二种归约为文献中已解决的博弈问题；第三种通过分析具有几何结构的更一般博弈来获得部分结果。

Result: 对于第一种变体，得到了完整的闭式均衡解；第二种变体成功归约为已知问题；第三种变体获得了部分结果。

Conclusion: 该博弈论框架不仅丰富了经典问答问题的理论，还为国家安全中的侦察任务、核浓缩证据检查等操作搜索问题提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: We consider a quiz show game in which a contestant is presented with a sequence of questions. Each time the contestant answers a question correctly, she receives a prize and proceeds to the next question; the probability of answering each question correctly is given. If the contestant answers a question incorrectly, she receives a consolation prize and the game ends. The contestant's problem of determining the optimal order in which to answer questions, for known fixed parameters, is a classic one studied in Kadane (1969), and admits a simple index-based solution. We consider game-theoretic versions of this problem in which a game show host can choose how to allocate a fixed prize budget. Our models are motivated by operational search problems in national security involving reconnaissance missions and inspecting for evidence of nuclear enrichment, as well as certain scheduling problems. We study three variants of the game, corresponding to different ways in which the host can distribute the prize money. For the first variant, we provide complete closed-form solutions, including equilibrium strategies and the value of the game. We reduce the second variant to a game solved in the literature. For the third variant, we obtain partial results by analyzing a more general game with a geometric structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Adaptive Fluid Cohomology on Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12001)
*Bastian Abt,David Stotko,Nils Wandel,Reinhard Klein*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应流体上同调（Adaptive Fluid Cohomology）框架，通过动态空间和时间细化来模拟无粘不可压缩流体在非单连通曲面上的流动，利用后验误差估计实现空间自适应，采用Dormand-Prince 5(4)时间步进方案，并开发了在网格突变时稳健传递谐波基的新方法。实验表明该方法在2D表面流动中可减少高达86%的内存占用，并在低质量网格上保持数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非单连通曲面上模拟无粘不可压缩流体时，现有方法缺乏空间和时间自适应性，且低质量网格上的数值不稳定性和对谐波分量的破坏限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出Adaptive Fluid Cohomology框架：1) 利用后验误差估计实现空间分辨率的动态调整；2) 采用Dormand-Prince 5(4)时间步进方案保证时间精度；3) 开发了网格重划分时稳健传递谐波基的新方法以确保稳定性。

Result: 在2D表面流动实验中，该方法能够准确再现高分辨率模拟的动力学特性，内存占用减少高达86%，且在低质量三角剖分网格上仍能保持数值稳定性，而静态方法在此条件下失败。

Conclusion: 自适应流体上同调框架有效解决了非单连通曲面上流体模拟的自适应性和稳定性问题，理论公式可推广至3D场景。

Abstract: Simulating inviscid, incompressible fluids on non-simply-connected curved surfaces requires careful treatment of the flow's local and global behavior. While recent theoretical advancements have established the critical dynamics of the harmonic component in such flows, practical applications remain computationally restricted by a lack of spatial and temporal adaptivity. Furthermore, simulations on poor-quality meshes often lead to numerical instability and a failure to preserve the flow's underlying harmonic component when using naive interpolation methods. In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Fluid Cohomology, a framework that integrates dynamic spatial and temporal refinement into the simulation of the Euler equations. We leverage a posteriori error estimation to adjust spatial resolution on the fly, alongside a standard Dormand-Prince 5(4) time-stepping scheme for temporal accuracy. To ensure stability during mesh mutations, we develop a novel method that robustly transfers the harmonic basis during remeshing. While our experimental evaluation focuses on 2D surface flows, the underlying theoretical formulation is presented to capture the 3D setting as well. Our evaluation demonstrates that this adaptive approach accurately recreates the dynamics of high-resolution simulations while reducing the memory footprint by up to 86% and maintaining numerical stability even on poor-quality triangulations where static methods fail.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Singular and nearly singular quadrature in arbitrary dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12440)
*Jing Hu,Luqiang He,Shuyu Sun*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯超几何函数变量变换技术的系统性方法，用于高精度计算任意维度下的奇异和近奇异积分。通过分析Duffy域和物理Cartesian域中的奇异结构，建立了基于sinh变换的统一处理框架。数值实验表明，sinh变换(β=1)相比其他超几何变换(β=2,3)具有更优的收敛性能，在二至五维空间中实现了指数收敛，显著优于直接Gauss-Legendre求积方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间中奇异和近奇异积分的精确数值评估是科学计算中的根本性挑战。现有方法（如奇异性提取、自适应单元细分、解析积分方案）在一维和二维空间中取得了成功，但推广到高维时面临严重的近奇异行为及数值不稳定性问题。亟需一种系统性的、适用于任意维度的统一处理方法。

Method: 基于高斯超几何函数的变量变换技术，在Duffy域和物理Cartesian域中分析奇异性与近奇异性结构，建立sinh变换的统一处理框架。通过Bernstein椭圆参数进行收敛性分析，比较不同β值（1,2,3）的超几何变换性能。

Result: 数值实验在二至五维空间中验证了该方法，sinh变换(β=1)实现指数收敛且显著优于直接Gauss-Legendre求积。收敛性分析揭示β=2,3的变换会引入二次近奇异性。同时分析了变分离散化中求积诱导的一致性误差。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维近奇异积分提供了一种实用且具有理论基础的框架，具有广泛适用性。sinh变换是最优选择，能够实现指数收敛并避免其他变换引入的二次近奇异性问题。

Abstract: The accurate numerical evaluation of integrals with singular or nearly singular kernels remains a fundamental challenge in scientific computing, particularly as the spatial dimension increases. While existing approaches, including singularity extraction, adaptive element subdivision, and analytical integration schemes, have achieved considerable success in one and two dimensions, their extension to higher dimensions is often hindered by severe near-singular behaviour and the resulting numerical instabilities. In this work, we propose a systematic approach for the high-accuracy evaluation of singular integrals and nearly singular integrals in arbitrary dimensions, based on variable transformation techniques derived from the Gauss hypergeometric function. We further analyze the structure of singularities and near-singularities in both the Duffy domain and the physical Cartesian domain, and establish a unified treatment via the sinh transformation. A convergence analysis based on the Bernstein ellipse parameter reveals that the sinh transformation ($β=1$) consistently outperforms alternative hypergeometric transformations ($β=2,3$), which introduce secondary near-singularities despite achieving formal cancellation. Numerical experiments in dimensions two through five, together with an analysis of quadrature-induced consistency errors in variational discretisations, demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves exponential convergence and substantially outperforms direct Gauss--Legendre quadrature. The method offers a practical and theoretically grounded framework for high-dimensional nearly singular integration with broad applicability in scientific computing.

</details>


### [212] [A 2.5D NURBS-Trace Infinite-Element Method for Moving-Load Wave Propagation and Soil--Structure Interaction in Semi-Infinite Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12582)
*Yanhui Zhong,Hao Hong,Bei Zhang,Quansheng Zang,Hussein Rappel,Stéphane P. A. Bordas*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种2.5D NURBS轨迹无限元方法（NBIEM），用于线性粘弹性半无限岩土介质中移动荷载引起的波传播问题。该方法将有限近场等几何分析与远场张量积指数径向函数结合，无需投影或mortar变量即可实现位移连续性。通过闭式径向矩评估远场刚度和质量贡献，消除了径向截断和数值积分。在亚Rayleigh、超剪切-亚压缩和超压缩移动荷载 regimes下验证了方法的准确性，并展示了在层状介质、轨道-路基系统和埋地结构中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对几何和材料性质沿传播方向近似不变的移动荷载问题，传统三维离散化计算成本过高，而2.5D分析能以较低成本保留三个位移分量。现有方法在远场处理、几何一致性和计算效率方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出耦合有限/无限元方案的2.5D NBIEM：近场采用等几何分析（NURBS）离散化，远场由边界NURBS基与出射或衰减指数径向函数的张量积表示。两个子域共享相同NURBS轨迹空间和控制点自由度，通过闭式径向矩计算远场贡献。

Result: 在亚Rayleigh、超剪切-亚压缩和超压缩移动荷载 regimes下准确验证了位移和应力频响函数。低频率研究评估了对径向参数和人工边界位置的敏感性。额外测试检验了复值响应精度、相位保真度、计算成本和默认S波外实现的频率相关工作范围。

Conclusion: 该方法为半无限域中移动荷载波传播和土-结构相互作用提供了几何一致且计算高效的处理框架，能够处理非均匀材料、多片配置、弯曲界面和覆盖层深度相关的岩土响应。

Abstract: For moving-load problems whose geometry and material properties are approximately invariant along the traveling direction, 2.5D analysis retains three displacement components at lower cost than full three-dimensional discretization. We present a 2.5D Non-Uniform Rational B-spline (NURBS)-trace infinite-element method (NBIEM), formulated as a coupled finite/infinite-element scheme, for wave propagation in linear viscoelastic semi-infinite geotechnical media. The bounded near field is discretized by isogeometric analysis, while the exterior is represented by tensor products of the boundary NURBS basis and admissible outgoing or evanescent exponential radial functions. Both subdomains share the same NURBS trace space and control-point degrees of freedom, enforcing displacement continuity without projection or mortar variables. For the selected radial functions, far-field stiffness and mass contributions are evaluated through closed-form radial moments, eliminating finite radial cutoff and radial quadrature. Closed-form half-space solutions verify displacement and stress frequency-response functions in sub-Rayleigh, super-shear but sub-compressional, and super-compressional moving-load regimes. Low-frequency studies assess sensitivity to radial parameters and artificial-boundary placement. Additional tests examine complex-valued response accuracy, phase fidelity, computational cost, and the frequency-dependent working range of the default S-wave-informed exterior realization. Applications to layered media, track--subgrade systems, and buried structures demonstrate the ability to handle heterogeneous materials, multi-patch configurations, curved interfaces, and cover-depth-dependent geotechnical responses. The framework provides a geometrically consistent and computationally efficient treatment of moving-load wave propagation and soil--structure interaction in semi-infinite domains.

</details>
